Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on poincare structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 956. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 618 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 956 + O(1/n^618). Summing over all modes and applying the poincare convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
